    def test_p10_old_samples_and_seed42_reports_are_byte_identical(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            for name, recipe, equipment in (
                ("sample_events", None, None), ("sample_events_v2", "lamp_models.json", None),
                ("sample_events_retest", "lamp_models_retest.json", None),
                ("sample_events_equipment", "lamp_models_retest.json", "equipment.json"),
                ("sample_events_restart", "lamp_models_retest.json", "equipment_restart.json"),
            ):
                args = [ROOT / "data" / (name + ".csv")]
                if recipe:
                    args += ["--recipe", ROOT / "recipes" / recipe]
                if equipment:
                    args += ["--equipment", ROOT / "recipes" / equipment]
                if name == "sample_events_restart":
                    args += ["--summary", "--trace", "f"]
                self.assert_success(cli(directory, "src.main", *args))
            generated = Path(directory) / "gen_seed42.csv"
            generate(42, 500, generated, ROOT / "recipes/generator.json")
            for suffix in ("", ".meta.json"):
                self.assertEqual(Path(str(generated) + suffix).read_bytes(),
                                 (ROOT / "data/generated" / ("gen_seed42.csv" + suffix)).read_bytes())
            self.assert_success(cli(directory, "src.main", generated, "--recipe", RECIPE,
                                    "--equipment", ROOT / "recipes/equipment_generated.json", "--summary",
                                    "--trace", "GEN_0005", "--trace", "GEN_0030"))
            print("P10 old sample/seed42 reports byte-identical:", self.compare_reports(directory), "; CSV/meta: 2")

    def test_p11_old_evaluation_reports_are_byte_identical(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            for out, config, seeds, rules, extra in (
                ("eval_dev", "generator_restart.json", "0-99", "3:2,5:2,5:3,10:3,10:4,20:4", ()),
                ("eval_holdout", "generator_restart.json", "1000-1099", "3:2", ("--holdout",)),
                ("eval_baseline", "generator.json", "0-99", "3:2", ("--holdout",)),
            ):
                self.assert_success(cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--config", ROOT / "recipes" / config,
                                        "--seeds", seeds, "--rules", rules, "--out", out, *extra))
            print("P11 old evaluation reports byte-identical:", self.compare_reports(directory))

    def test_p12_submitted_lot_evaluation_and_sample_reports_reproduce(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            self.assert_success(cli(directory, "src.main", ROOT / "data/sample_events_lot.csv", "--recipe", RECIPE,
                                    "--equipment", EQUIPMENT, "--summary", "--containment"))
            for setting in ("lot", "lot_clustered"):
                config = ROOT / "recipes" / ("generator_" + setting + ".json")
                dev = "eval_" + setting + "_dev"
                self.assert_success(cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--containment", "--config", config,
                                        "--seeds", "0-99", "--rules", "3:2", "--out", dev))
                self.assert_success(cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--containment", "--config", config,
                                        "--seeds", "1000-1099", "--rules", "3:2", "--out",
                                        "eval_" + setting + "_holdout", "--holdout", "--selected-from",
                                        Path(directory) / "reports" / (dev + "_containment.csv")))
            print("P12 submitted lot reports byte-identical:", self.compare_reports(directory))

    def test_p13_t81_meta_and_full_reports_reproduce(self):
        for seed, products in ((42, 500), (7, 200)):
            with self.subTest(seed=seed), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
                snapshots = []
                for repeat in ("a", "b"):
                    working = Path(directory) / repeat
                    working.mkdir()
                    output = working / "generated.csv"
                    generate(seed, products, output, CONFIG)
                    self.assert_success(cli(working, "src.main", output, "--recipe", RECIPE,
                                            "--equipment", EQUIPMENT, "--summary", "--containment"))
                    snapshots.append({p.relative_to(working).as_posix(): p.read_bytes()
                                      for p in working.rglob("*") if p.is_file()})
                self.assertEqual(snapshots[0], snapshots[1])
                self.assertEqual(len(snapshots[0]), 8)
        print("P13 T81 CSV/meta/6 reports reproduce: seeds 42/500 and 7/200")

import hashlib
import copy
from collections import Counter
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from src.generate import GENERATOR_VERSION, Generator, generate
from src.equipment import load_equipment
from src.main import read_events
from src.recipe import load_recipe
from src.recipe_rules import EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS, RecipeProcess
from src.summary import build_analysis, build_summary


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes/generator.json"


class GeneratorTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_t51_defect_probability_applies_once_to_initial_and_retest_results(self):
        models = load_recipe(ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json").models
        book = load_equipment(ROOT / "recipes/equipment_generated.json", models)
        config = json.loads(CONFIG.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        cases = (
            ([0.019, 0.899, 0.69, 0.899, 0.69, 0.899], ["FAIL"] * 3, ["p"]),
            ([0.019, 0.9, 0.9, 0.9], ["PASS"] * 3, ["p"]),
            ([0.02, 0.02, 0.02, 0.02], ["PASS"] * 3, []),
        )
        for draws, expected, defective in cases:
            with self.subTest(draws=draws):
                generator = Generator(42, config, models, book)
                with patch.object(generator.rng, "random", side_effect=draws):
                    rows = [row for row, _ in generator.product("p", "A")]
                results = [row for row in rows if row["event"] == "INSPECT"]
                self.assertEqual([row["result"] for row in results], expected)
                self.assertEqual(generator.defective_products, defective)
                self.assertTrue(all(tuple(row) == EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS for row in rows))

    def test_t51_seed42_meta_lists_defects_without_csv_truth_columns(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            target = Path(directory) / "events.csv"
            meta = generate(42, 500, target, CONFIG)
            defective = meta["defective_products"]
            self.assertEqual(meta["defective_count"], len(defective))
            self.assertEqual(len(defective), len(set(defective)))
            self.assertGreater(len(defective), 0)
            events = read_events(target, EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS)
            registered = {row["product_id"] for row in events if row["event"] == "REGISTER"}
            self.assertTrue(set(defective) <= registered)
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(Path(str(target) + ".meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")), meta)

    def test_t42_same_seed_csv_meta_and_provenance(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            a, b = Path(directory) / "a.csv", Path(directory) / "b.csv"
            first = generate(42, 500, a, CONFIG)
            generate(42, 500, b, CONFIG)
            self.assertEqual(a.read_bytes(), b.read_bytes())
            self.assertEqual(Path(str(a) + ".meta.json").read_bytes(),
                             Path(str(b) + ".meta.json").read_bytes())
            self.assertEqual((first["seed"], first["products"], first["generator_version"]),
                             (42, 500, GENERATOR_VERSION))
            for key, path in (("config", CONFIG), ("recipe", ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json"),
                              ("equipment", ROOT / "recipes/equipment_generated.json")):
                self.assertEqual(first["sha256"][key], hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest())
            self.assertEqual(first["rows"], first["normal_rows"] + sum(first["injected_errors"].values()))
            counts = list(first["injected_errors"].values())
            self.assertLessEqual(max(counts) - min(counts), 1)
            self.assertEqual(sum(counts), int(first["normal_rows"] * 0.03))

    def test_t43_different_seed_changes_csv(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            a, b = Path(directory) / "a.csv", Path(directory) / "b.csv"
            generate(42, 500, a, CONFIG)
            generate(43, 500, b, CONFIG)
            self.assertNotEqual(a.read_bytes(), b.read_bytes())

    def test_review005_m3_missing_candidate_explains_failure_without_output(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            target = Path(directory) / "small.csv"
            result = subprocess.run(
                ["py", "-m", "src.generate", "--seed", "39", "--products", "4", "--out", str(target),
                 "--config", str(CONFIG)], cwd=directory, capture_output=True,
                env={**os.environ, "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"})
            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr.decode("utf-8"))

class PreservationChecks(unittest.TestCase):
    def assert_success(self, result):
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)

    def compare_reports(self, directory):
        paths = sorted((Path(directory) / "reports").glob("*.csv"))
        self.assertTrue(paths)
        for path in paths:
            self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), (ROOT / "reports" / path.name).read_bytes(), path.name)
        return len(paths)

    def test_p09_protected_tracked_files_unchanged(self):
        paths = subprocess.check_output(["git", "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", BASE,
                                         "tests", "src/rules.py", "recipes", "data", "reports"], cwd=ROOT,
                                        text=True, encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        changed = subprocess.check_output(["git", "diff", "--name-only", BASE, "--", *paths], cwd=ROOT,
                                          text=True, encoding="utf-8")
| 미완료 제품 수(보류·폐기 포함) | 최소 10, 중앙값 22, 최대 417 |
| 보류 제품 평균 / 그중 정상품 평균 | 13.73 / 8.50 |

## 보존 조건 (가장 먼저 지킬 것)
- 설비 파일에 `restart_enabled: true`가 없으면 v1·1–4단계의 동작·출력·종료 코드는 바이트까지 같다.
- `recipes/generator.json`, `recipes/equipment.json`, `recipes/equipment_generated.json`과 기존 샘플·seed42 산출물은 바꾸지 않는다.
- `src/rules.py`와 기존 테스트 6개 파일은 수정하지 않는다. 5단계 테스트는 `tests/test_restart.py`, `tests/test_evaluate.py`에 둔다.
- `GENERATOR_VERSION`은 `1.1.0`을 유지한다. 재가동 설정의 생성에만 새 상수 `RESTART_GENERATOR_VERSION = "1.2.0"`을 meta에 기록한다.
- `Analysis.stops`는 설비별 **첫 정지** dict로 유지한다(기존 테스트 사용). 정지 이력은 새 필드 `stop_history`에 둔다.
- `EquipmentBook` 새 필드는 기본값이 있는 뒤쪽 필드로 추가한다.

## 재가동 모드 켜기
- 설비 파일 최상위에 선택 키 `"restart_enabled": true`를 둔다. 값은 bool만 허용하며, 그 밖의 값은 설비 오류(종료 코드 3)다.
- 새 파일 `recipes/equipment_restart.json`: 설비 목록은 `equipment_generated.json`과 같고 `stop_rule`은 `{"window": 3, "fails": 2}`, `restart_enabled`는 `true`다.
- 재가동 모드에서 레시피 공정 ID가 `EQUIP_RESTART`이면 설비 오류(종료 코드 3)다.

- 생성 확률(예: LEAK-2 30%)은 **심어 놓은 가정**이다. 생성 데이터의 결과는 실제 설비 고장 진단이나 공정 개선의 증거가 아니다. README와 summary 출력에 이 내용을 고정 문장으로 표시한다.
- 제외: 그래프, 외부 패키지, 헤더·CSV 구문 오류 생성(전체 실행을 중단시키므로), 설비 재가동.

## 보존 조건 (가장 먼저 지킬 것)
- v1·1·2·3단계 동작·출력·테스트는 바뀌지 않는다. `--summary`·`--trace`가 없으면 3단계까지와 완전히 같다.
- `src/rules.py`와 기존 테스트 4개 파일(`test_rules.py`, `test_recipe.py`, `test_retest.py`, `test_equipment.py`)은 수정하지 않는다. 4단계 테스트는 `tests/test_generate.py`, `tests/test_summary.py`에 둔다.
- 기존 샘플 보고서는 다시 만들었을 때 체크아웃된 파일과 같아야 한다.
- 리뷰 004 L1: 설비 모드를 보고서 개수(`len(reports) == 4`)로 추정하는 구조에 summary를 덧붙이지 않는다. summary·trace는 **별도 경로**로 계산·저장하고, 모드는 명시적인 값으로 전달한다. 이 때문에 기존 출력이 바뀌면 안 된다.

## 생성기
- 실행: `py -m src.generate --seed 42 --products 500 --out data/generated/gen_seed42.csv` (기본값: `--seed 42`, `--products 500`, `--config recipes/generator.json`)
- 사용하는 설정: 레시피 `recipes/lamp_models_retest.json`, 설비 `recipes/equipment_generated.json`. 둘 다 `generator.json`에 경로로 적는다.
- 난수는 `random.Random(seed)` 하나만 쓴다. 시각·환경을 쓰지 않는다. **재현 기준은 같은 시드 + 같은 설정 파일 + 같은 생성기 버전**이다. 출력 옆에 `<out>.meta.json`을 쓴다: 시드, 제품 수, 설정·레시피·설비 파일의 SHA-256, 생성기 버전 상수, 생성 행 수, 주입 오류 종류별 개수.
- `recipes/equipment_generated.json`: `stop_rule`은 3단계와 같은 `{"window": 3, "fails": 2}`. 설비는 `LEAK-1`, `LEAK-2`, `LEAK-3`, `LIGHT-1`, `LIGHT-2`, `FINAL-1`, `FINAL-2`. (3단계 `equipment.json`은 바꾸지 않는다.)
- 정지 기준(최근 3건 중 2건 FAIL)은 SPC 관리도의 Western Electric 규칙 중 "연속 3점 중 2점" 패턴을 PASS/FAIL 데이터에 맞게 단순화한 **가상 규칙**이다. 실제 SL 기준이나 통계적 관리한계가 아니다.
- 이번 단계 제외: 설비 재가동·점검 이벤트, 로트 ID, 공정 설비, 정지 원인 FAIL 제품의 자동 재검사 요청, 측정값·관리한계 계산.

## 보존 조건 (가장 먼저 지킬 것)
- v1, 1단계, 2단계의 동작·출력·테스트는 바뀌지 않는다. `--equipment`가 없으면 2단계까지와 완전히 같다.
- `src/rules.py`, `tests/test_rules.py`, `tests/test_recipe.py`, `tests/test_retest.py`는 수정하지 않는다. 3단계 테스트는 `tests/test_equipment.py`에 둔다.
- 기존 샘플 보고서(`reports/sample_events_*.csv`, `_v2_*`, `_retest_*`)는 다시 만들었을 때 체크아웃된 파일과 같아야 한다.

## 실행
- `py -m src.main <입력CSV> --recipe <레시피JSON> --equipment <설비JSON>`
- `--equipment`는 `--recipe`와 함께만 쓴다. 없이 쓰면 종료 코드 2. 재검사 레시피와 함께 쓰면 재검사 규칙도 그대로 적용된다.
- 기본 설비 파일: `recipes/equipment.json`

## 설비 파일 형식
- 모든 규칙은 가상 규칙이다. 재검사 상한 2회는 실제 SL 기준이 아니다.
- 이번 단계 제외: 설비·로트(3단계), 대량 생성기(4단계), 요청 ID·시도 ID 열 추가, 재검사 설비 지정, 폐기 취소, 레시피별로 다른 상한.

## 보존 조건 (가장 먼저 지킬 것)
- v1: 기존 조건 그대로(T1–T10, v1 보고서 동일).
- 1단계: `--recipe recipes/lamp_models.json`(재검사 설정 없음)의 동작·출력은 바뀌지 않는다. **T11–T22 테스트는 수정하지 않고 통과**해야 하며, `reports/sample_events_v2_*.csv` 3종도 다시 만들었을 때 체크아웃된 파일과 같아야 한다.
- `src/rules.py`, `tests/test_rules.py`, `tests/test_recipe.py`는 수정하지 않는다. 2단계 테스트는 새 파일(`tests/test_retest.py`)에 둔다.

## 재검사 모드 켜기
- 레시피의 **모든 모델**에 `"retest_limit": 2`를 넣으면 재검사 모드다. 하나도 없으면 1단계와 똑같이 동작한다.
- 일부 모델에만 있으면 설정 오류다. `retest_limit`은 1–5 정수(`true`·문자열 불가)여야 한다. 위반은 기존 레시피 검증과 같이 모든 오류를 표시하고 종료 코드 3.
- 기본 재검사 레시피: `recipes/lamp_models_retest.json` (`lamp_models.json`과 같고 두 모델에 `"retest_limit": 2`만 추가).
- `RETEST_REQUEST`는 공정 ID로 쓸 수 없는 예약어에 추가한다(`REGISTER`, `INSPECT`와 같음).
- 모든 공정·검사·모델은 **가상 규칙**이다. SL 실제 공정·기준이 아니다. 최종 상태 "레시피 모의 흐름 완료"는 출하·양품·SL 기준 적합을 뜻하지 않는다.
- 이번 단계 제외: 재검사·보류 해제(2단계), 설비·로트(3단계), 대량 생성기(4단계), 분기·병렬·순환 공정(그래프), 검사 통과를 다음 공정 조건으로 거는 중간 관문, 실행 중 레시피 변경, 등록 정정, 외부 패키지.

## v1 보존 (가장 먼저 지킬 조건)
- `py -m src.main <CSV>`(옵션 없음)는 v1과 **완전히 같게** 동작한다. 입력 5열, 보고서 3종, 규칙 ID 5개, 상태명, 종료 코드 모두 그대로다.
- `src/rules.py`와 `tests/test_rules.py`는 수정하지 않는다. v2 판정은 새 모듈(예: `src/recipe.py`, `src/recipe_rules.py`)에 둔다. 공통으로 쓸 수 있는 작은 함수만 옮기는 것도 하지 않는다(v1 회귀 위험 차단).
- 회귀 확인: T1–T10 무수정 통과, `py -m src.main data/sample_events.csv`로 다시 만든 `reports/sample_events_*.csv` 3종이 커밋된 파일과 바이트 단위로 같다.

## 실행
- v2: `py -m src.main <입력CSV> --recipe <레시피JSON>`
- 테스트: `py -m unittest discover -s tests` (v1·v2 테스트 모두)
- 기본 레시피 파일: `recipes/lamp_models.json`

+"""Review 013: focused adversarial checks and required preservation checks.
+
+Run: py -m unittest handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_013.py
+Generated files are confined to TemporaryDirectory; no prior probe is imported.
+"""
+import copy
+import csv
+from dataclasses import replace
+from fractions import Fraction
+import hashlib
+import json
+import math
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+
+from src.containment import METHODS, build_containment, run_scopes
+from src.equipment import load_equipment
+from src.evaluate import containment_metrics
+from src.generate import check_lot_config, generate, load_setup
+from src.recipe import load_recipe
+from src.recipe_rules import LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS
+from src.summary import build_analysis
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+BASE = "d12e92e48c9cfc0bc716d54a39d169120ca581f8"
+RECIPE = ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json"
+EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes/equipment_lot.json"
+CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes/generator_lot.json"
+
+
+def cli(directory, module, *args):
+    return subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, "-m", module, *map(str, args)], cwd=directory,
+        env={**os.environ, "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1",
+             "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"}, capture_output=True, text=True,
+        encoding="utf-8", timeout=300)
+
+
+def read_csv(path):
+    with Path(path).open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
+        return list(csv.DictReader(source))
+
+
+def fixture(directory, **changes):
+    data = json.loads(CONFIG.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+    data.update(recipe=str(RECIPE), equipment=str(EQUIPMENT))
+    data.update(changes)
+    path = Path(directory) / "config.json"
+    path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
+    return path
+
+
+def event(pid, name, result="", lot="", model="", seq=1):
+    return dict(zip(LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS, (str(seq), pid, name,
+                    "LEAK" if name == "INSPECT" else "", result, model,
+                    "LEAK-1" if name == "INSPECT" else "", lot)))
+
+
+def prepared(*ids):
+    return [item for pid in ids for item in (
+        event(pid, "REGISTER", lot="L1", model="B"), event(pid, "P1"), event(pid, "P2"))]
+
+
+class FocusedProbes(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls):
+        cls.models = load_recipe(RECIPE).models
+        cls.book = load_equipment(EQUIPMENT, cls.models)
+
+    def success(self, result):
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+
+    def test_p01_empty_scopes_mode_combinations(self):
+        for lot in (False, True):
+            for restart in (False, True):
+                with self.subTest(lot=lot, restart=restart):
+                    book = replace(self.book, lot_tracking=lot, restart_enabled=restart)
+                    analysis = build_analysis([], self.models, book)
+                    result = run_scopes(analysis)
+                    expected = [m for m in METHODS if lot or m != "lot"]
+                    self.assertEqual(list(result), expected)
+                    self.assertTrue(all(value == set() for value in result.values()))
+                    self.assertEqual(build_containment(analysis), [])
+                    # The empty sets must not share storage across methods or calls.
+                    result["window"].add("sentinel")
+                    self.assertTrue(all(not result[m] for m in expected if m != "window"))
+                    self.assertTrue(all(not s for s in run_scopes(analysis).values()))
+        print("P01: four lot/restart combinations; independent empty sets; no report rows")
+
+    def test_p02_no_stop_with_actual_leaks_retains_denominator(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            config = fixture(directory, error_rate=0, defective_product_probability=1,
+                             defective_fail_probability=0, normal_fail_probability=0,
+                             faulty_equipment={"LEAK-2": 0})
+            self.success(cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--containment", "--config", config,
+                             "--rules", "3:2", "--seeds", "0-1", "--products", "12", "--out", "dev"))
+            rows = read_csv(Path(directory) / "reports/dev_containment.csv")
+            methods = [r for r in rows if r["section"] == "method"]
+            self.assertEqual([r["method"] for r in methods], list(METHODS))
+            for row in methods:
+                self.assertGreater(int(row["leaks"]), 0)
+                self.assertEqual((row["runs"], row["leak_runs"], row["stops"]), ("2", "2", "0"))
+                self.assertEqual((row["capture_rate"], row["precision"], row["mean_quarantine"]),
+                                 ("0.0000", "", "0.00"))
+                self.assertEqual(Fraction(row["mean_residual"]), Fraction(int(row["leaks"]), 2))
+            print("P02: no stops, leaks=" + methods[0]["leaks"] + "; capture=0.0000; residual=" + methods[0]["mean_residual"])
+
+    def test_p03_every_selected_method_without_stops(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            config = fixture(directory, error_rate=0, defective_product_probability=0,
+                             normal_fail_probability=0, faulty_equipment={"LEAK-2": 0})
+            chosen = Path(directory) / "selection.csv"
+            sha = hashlib.sha256(config.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+            for method in METHODS:
+                with self.subTest(method=method):
+                    with chosen.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
+                        writer = csv.DictWriter(target, fieldnames=("section", "method", "value"))
+                        writer.writeheader()
+                        writer.writerows([{"section": "provenance", "method": "config_sha256", "value": sha},
+                                          {"section": "selection", "method": method, "value": "선택"}])
+                    self.success(cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--containment", "--config", config,
+                                     "--rules", "3:2", "--seeds", "1000-1000", "--products", "1",
+                                     "--out", method, "--holdout", "--selected-from", chosen))
+                    rows = read_csv(Path(directory) / "reports" / (method + "_containment.csv"))
+                    self.assertEqual([r["method"] for r in rows if r["section"] == "method"], list(METHODS))
+                    self.assertEqual([(r["method"], r["value"]) for r in rows if r["section"] == "holdout_verdict"],
+                                     [(method, "미확인")])
+                    self.assertFalse(any(r["section"] == "selection" for r in rows))
+            print("P03: all five selected methods exit 0 / unconfirmed; no reselection")
+
+    def test_p04_mixed_runs_rejection_and_scope_purity(self):
+        rows = prepared("a", "b", "c") + [event("a", "INSPECT", "PASS"),
+                event("b", "INSPECT", "FAIL"), event("c", "INSPECT", "FAIL")]
+        stopped = build_analysis(rows, self.models, self.book)
+        no_stop_rows = prepared("d") + [event("d", "INSPECT", "FAIL", lot="bad"),
+                                       event("d", "INSPECT", "PASS")]
+        no_stop = build_analysis(no_stop_rows, self.models, self.book)
+        self.assertEqual([d.accepted for d in no_stop.decisions[-2:]], [False, True])
+        before = copy.deepcopy((no_stop.process.products, no_stop.process.equipment))
+        empty = run_scopes(no_stop)
+        self.assertEqual((no_stop.process.products, no_stop.process.equipment), before)
+        result = containment_metrics([({"a"}, run_scopes(stopped)), ({"d"}, empty)])
+        for method in METHODS:
+            self.assertEqual(result[method]["capture_rate"], Fraction(1, 2))
+            self.assertEqual(result[method]["mean_quarantine"], Fraction(1, 2))
+            self.assertEqual(result[method]["mean_residual"], Fraction(1, 2))
+        print("P04: rejected FAIL does not stop; mixed runs capture/quarantine/residual=1/2; state unchanged")
+
+    def test_p05_huge_signed_integers_in_all_three_probabilities(self):
+        for key in ("faulty_detection_loss", "defective_lot_probability", "defective_in_lot_probability"):
+            for value in (10 ** 400, -(10 ** 400)):
+                with self.subTest(key=key, positive=value > 0), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+                    changes = {"defective_lot_probability": 0.25, "defective_in_lot_probability": 0.2}
+                    changes[key] = {"LEAK-2": value} if key == "faulty_detection_loss" else value
+                    config = fixture(directory, **changes)
+                    results = [cli(directory, "src.generate", "--config", config, "--seed", "0",
+                                   "--products", "5", "--out", "gen.csv"),
+                               cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--containment", "--config", config,
+                                   "--rules", "3:2", "--seeds", "0-0", "--products", "5", "--out", "bad")]
+                    for result in results:
+                        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr)
+                        self.assertNotIn("Traceback", result.stderr)
+                    self.assertEqual({p.name for p in Path(directory).iterdir()}, {"config.json"})
+        print("P05: +/- 10**400 in all three probability fields; 12 CLI errors exit 2; no output")
+
+    def test_p06_nonfinite_types_and_nearest_valid_boundaries(self):
+        config, _, _, book = load_setup(CONFIG)
+        keys = ("faulty_detection_loss", "defective_lot_probability", "defective_in_lot_probability")
+        invalid = (math.nan, math.inf, -math.inf, True, False, None, "0.5", [], {},
+                   math.nextafter(0.0, -math.inf), math.nextafter(1.0, math.inf))
+        valid = (0, 1, -0.0, math.nextafter(0.0, 1.0), math.nextafter(1.0, 0.0))
+        for key in keys:
+            for value in (*invalid, *valid):
+                with self.subTest(key=key, value=repr(value)):
+                    candidate = {**config, "defective_lot_probability": 0.25, "defective_in_lot_probability": 0.2}
+                    candidate[key] = {"LEAK-2": value} if key == "faulty_detection_loss" else value
+                    if any(value is item for item in invalid):
+                        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+                            check_lot_config(candidate, book)
+                    else:
+                        check_lot_config(candidate, book)
+        print("P06: 33 invalid type/nonfinite/outside-boundary cases rejected; 15 valid boundary cases accepted")
+
+    def test_p07_rejected_configuration_then_valid_run_same_destination(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            config = fixture(directory, faulty_detection_loss={"LEAK-2": -(10 ** 400)})
+            args = ("--config", config, "--seed", "7", "--products", "20", "--out", "gen.csv")
+            bad = cli(directory, "src.generate", *args)
+            self.assertEqual(bad.returncode, 2, bad.stderr)
+            config = fixture(directory, error_rate=0)

+            self.success(cli(directory, "src.main", Path(directory) / "gen.csv", "--recipe", RECIPE,
+                             "--equipment", EQUIPMENT, "--summary", "--containment"))
+            rows = read_csv(Path(directory) / "reports/gen_rejections.csv")
+            self.assertEqual(rows, [])
+            self.assertEqual(json.loads((Path(directory) / "gen.csv.meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["generator_version"], "1.3.0")
+            print("P07: rejected config leaves no partial output; corrected config generates and judges successfully")

+class PreservationChecks(unittest.TestCase):
+    def success(self, result):
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+
+    def compare_reports(self, directory, expected):
+        paths = sorted((Path(directory) / "reports").glob("*.csv"))
+        self.assertEqual(len(paths), expected)
+        for path in paths:
+            self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), (ROOT / "reports" / path.name).read_bytes(), path.name)
+        return len(paths)
+
+    def test_p08_protected_tracked_files_unchanged(self):
+        paths = subprocess.check_output(["git", "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", BASE,
+                                         "tests", "src/rules.py", "recipes", "data", "reports"], cwd=ROOT,
+                                        text=True, encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
+        changes = subprocess.check_output(["git", "diff", "--name-only", BASE, "--", *paths], cwd=ROOT,
+                                          text=True, encoding="utf-8")
+        self.assertEqual(changes, "")
+        print("P08: protected pre-stage6 tracked files unchanged:", len(paths))
+
+    def test_p09_previous_modes_samples_and_seed42_bytes(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            for name, recipe, equipment in (
+                ("sample_events", None, None), ("sample_events_v2", "lamp_models.json", None),
+                ("sample_events_retest", "lamp_models_retest.json", None),
+                ("sample_events_equipment", "lamp_models_retest.json", "equipment.json"),
+                ("sample_events_restart", "lamp_models_retest.json", "equipment_restart.json")):
+                args = [ROOT / "data" / (name + ".csv")]
+                if recipe:
+                    args += ["--recipe", ROOT / "recipes" / recipe]
+                if equipment:
+                    args += ["--equipment", ROOT / "recipes" / equipment]
+                if name == "sample_events_restart":
+                    args += ["--summary", "--trace", "f"]
+                self.success(cli(directory, "src.main", *args))
+            generated = Path(directory) / "gen_seed42.csv"
+            generate(42, 500, generated, ROOT / "recipes/generator.json")
+            for suffix in ("", ".meta.json"):
+                self.assertEqual(Path(str(generated) + suffix).read_bytes(),
+                                 (ROOT / "data/generated" / ("gen_seed42.csv" + suffix)).read_bytes())
+            self.success(cli(directory, "src.main", generated, "--recipe", RECIPE,
+                             "--equipment", ROOT / "recipes/equipment_generated.json", "--summary",
+                             "--trace", "GEN_0005", "--trace", "GEN_0030"))
+            print("P09: existing reports byte-identical:", self.compare_reports(directory, 26), "; CSV/meta: 2")
+
+    def test_p10_previous_evaluation_report_bytes(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:

from .recipe_rules import RULE_IDS, RecipeProcess


PRODUCT_COLUMNS = ("product_id", "model", "state", "incomplete_reason")
RETEST_PRODUCT_COLUMNS = PRODUCT_COLUMNS + ("history",)
LOT_COLUMN_INDEX = 2
EQUIPMENT_COLUMNS = ("equipment", "inspection", "counted_results", "fail_count", "state", "stopped_at_seq")
RESTART_COLUMNS = EQUIPMENT_COLUMNS + ("stop_count", "restart_count", "last_restart_result")


def build_recipe_reports(events, models, equipment_book=None):
    process = RecipeProcess(models, equipment_book)
    rows = []
    rule_ids = RULE_IDS + ("RULE_RETEST",) if process.retest_enabled else RULE_IDS
    if equipment_book is not None:
        rule_ids += ("RULE_EQUIP",)
    counts = dict.fromkeys(rule_ids, 0)
    for event in events:
        decision = process.apply(event)
        rows.append((
            decision.seq,
            decision.product_id,
            decision.input_text,
            decision.rule_id,
            "허용" if decision.accepted else "거절",
            decision.reason,
            decision.state,
        ))
        if not decision.accepted:
            counts[decision.rule_id] += 1
    products = [
        (product_id, product.model)
        + ((product.lot,) if equipment_book is not None and equipment_book.lot_tracking else ())
        + (product.status, product.incomplete_reason)
        + ((product.history,) if process.retest_enabled else ())
        for product_id, product in process.products.items()
    ]
    reports = rows, products, list(counts.items())
    if equipment_book is not None:
        equipment = [
            (name, equipment_book.equipment[name], state.counted_results, state.fail_count,
             state.state, state.stopped_at_seq or "")
            + ((state.stop_count, state.restart_count, state.last_restart_result)
               if equipment_book.restart_enabled else ())
            for name, state in process.equipment.items()
        ]
        reports += (equipment,)
    return reports

+"""Review 013: focused adversarial checks and required preservation checks.
+
+Run: py -m unittest handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_013.py
+Generated files are confined to TemporaryDirectory; no prior probe is imported.
+"""
+import copy
+import csv
+from dataclasses import replace
+from fractions import Fraction
+import hashlib
+import json
+import math
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+
+from src.containment import METHODS, build_containment, run_scopes
+from src.equipment import load_equipment
+from src.evaluate import containment_metrics
+from src.generate import check_lot_config, generate, load_setup
+from src.recipe import load_recipe
+from src.recipe_rules import LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS
+from src.summary import build_analysis
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+BASE = "d12e92e48c9cfc0bc716d54a39d169120ca581f8"
+RECIPE = ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json"
+EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes/equipment_lot.json"
+CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes/generator_lot.json"
+
+
+def cli(directory, module, *args):
+    return subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, "-m", module, *map(str, args)], cwd=directory,
+        env={**os.environ, "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1",
+             "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"}, capture_output=True, text=True,
+        encoding="utf-8", timeout=300)
+
+
+def read_csv(path):
+    with Path(path).open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
+        return list(csv.DictReader(source))
+
+
+def fixture(directory, **changes):
+    data = json.loads(CONFIG.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+    data.update(recipe=str(RECIPE), equipment=str(EQUIPMENT))
+    data.update(changes)
+    path = Path(directory) / "config.json"
+    path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
+    return path
+
+
+def event(pid, name, result="", lot="", model="", seq=1):
+    return dict(zip(LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS, (str(seq), pid, name,
+                    "LEAK" if name == "INSPECT" else "", result, model,
+                    "LEAK-1" if name == "INSPECT" else "", lot)))
+
+
+def prepared(*ids):
+    return [item for pid in ids for item in (
+        event(pid, "REGISTER", lot="L1", model="B"), event(pid, "P1"), event(pid, "P2"))]
+
+
+class FocusedProbes(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls):
+        cls.models = load_recipe(RECIPE).models
+        cls.book = load_equipment(EQUIPMENT, cls.models)
+
+    def success(self, result):
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+
+    def test_p01_empty_scopes_mode_combinations(self):
+        for lot in (False, True):
+            for restart in (False, True):
+                with self.subTest(lot=lot, restart=restart):
+                    book = replace(self.book, lot_tracking=lot, restart_enabled=restart)
+                    analysis = build_analysis([], self.models, book)
+                    result = run_scopes(analysis)
+                    expected = [m for m in METHODS if lot or m != "lot"]
+                    self.assertEqual(list(result), expected)
+                    self.assertTrue(all(value == set() for value in result.values()))
+                    self.assertEqual(build_containment(analysis), [])
+                    # The empty sets must not share storage across methods or calls.
+                    result["window"].add("sentinel")
+                    self.assertTrue(all(not result[m] for m in expected if m != "window"))
+                    self.assertTrue(all(not s for s in run_scopes(analysis).values()))
+        print("P01: four lot/restart combinations; independent empty sets; no report rows")
+
+    def test_p02_no_stop_with_actual_leaks_retains_denominator(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            config = fixture(directory, error_rate=0, defective_product_probability=1,
+                             defective_fail_probability=0, normal_fail_probability=0,
+                             faulty_equipment={"LEAK-2": 0})
+            self.success(cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--containment", "--config", config,
+                             "--rules", "3:2", "--seeds", "0-1", "--products", "12", "--out", "dev"))
+            rows = read_csv(Path(directory) / "reports/dev_containment.csv")
+            methods = [r for r in rows if r["section"] == "method"]
+            self.assertEqual([r["method"] for r in methods], list(METHODS))
+            for row in methods:
+                self.assertGreater(int(row["leaks"]), 0)
+                self.assertEqual((row["runs"], row["leak_runs"], row["stops"]), ("2", "2", "0"))
+                self.assertEqual((row["capture_rate"], row["precision"], row["mean_quarantine"]),
+                                 ("0.0000", "", "0.00"))
+                self.assertEqual(Fraction(row["mean_residual"]), Fraction(int(row["leaks"]), 2))
+            print("P02: no stops, leaks=" + methods[0]["leaks"] + "; capture=0.0000; residual=" + methods[0]["mean_residual"])
+
+    def test_p03_every_selected_method_without_stops(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            config = fixture(directory, error_rate=0, defective_product_probability=0,
+                             normal_fail_probability=0, faulty_equipment={"LEAK-2": 0})
+            chosen = Path(directory) / "selection.csv"
+            sha = hashlib.sha256(config.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+            for method in METHODS:
+                with self.subTest(method=method):
+                    with chosen.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
+                        writer = csv.DictWriter(target, fieldnames=("section", "method", "value"))
+                        writer.writeheader()
+                        writer.writerows([{"section": "provenance", "method": "config_sha256", "value": sha},
+                                          {"section": "selection", "method": method, "value": "선택"}])
+                    self.success(cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--containment", "--config", config,
+                                     "--rules", "3:2", "--seeds", "1000-1000", "--products", "1",
+                                     "--out", method, "--holdout", "--selected-from", chosen))
+                    rows = read_csv(Path(directory) / "reports" / (method + "_containment.csv"))
+                    self.assertEqual([r["method"] for r in rows if r["section"] == "method"], list(METHODS))
+                    self.assertEqual([(r["method"], r["value"]) for r in rows if r["section"] == "holdout_verdict"],
+                                     [(method, "미확인")])
+                    self.assertFalse(any(r["section"] == "selection" for r in rows))
+            print("P03: all five selected methods exit 0 / unconfirmed; no reselection")
+
+    def test_p04_mixed_runs_rejection_and_scope_purity(self):
+        rows = prepared("a", "b", "c") + [event("a", "INSPECT", "PASS"),
+                event("b", "INSPECT", "FAIL"), event("c", "INSPECT", "FAIL")]
+        stopped = build_analysis(rows, self.models, self.book)
+        no_stop_rows = prepared("d") + [event("d", "INSPECT", "FAIL", lot="bad"),
+                                       event("d", "INSPECT", "PASS")]
+        no_stop = build_analysis(no_stop_rows, self.models, self.book)
+        self.assertEqual([d.accepted for d in no_stop.decisions[-2:]], [False, True])
+        before = copy.deepcopy((no_stop.process.products, no_stop.process.equipment))
+        empty = run_scopes(no_stop)
+        self.assertEqual((no_stop.process.products, no_stop.process.equipment), before)
+        result = containment_metrics([({"a"}, run_scopes(stopped)), ({"d"}, empty)])
+        for method in METHODS:
+            self.assertEqual(result[method]["capture_rate"], Fraction(1, 2))
+            self.assertEqual(result[method]["mean_quarantine"], Fraction(1, 2))
+            self.assertEqual(result[method]["mean_residual"], Fraction(1, 2))
+        print("P04: rejected FAIL does not stop; mixed runs capture/quarantine/residual=1/2; state unchanged")
+
+    def test_p05_huge_signed_integers_in_all_three_probabilities(self):
+        for key in ("faulty_detection_loss", "defective_lot_probability", "defective_in_lot_probability"):
+            for value in (10 ** 400, -(10 ** 400)):
+                with self.subTest(key=key, positive=value > 0), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+                    changes = {"defective_lot_probability": 0.25, "defective_in_lot_probability": 0.2}
+                    changes[key] = {"LEAK-2": value} if key == "faulty_detection_loss" else value
+                    config = fixture(directory, **changes)
+                    results = [cli(directory, "src.generate", "--config", config, "--seed", "0",
+                                   "--products", "5", "--out", "gen.csv"),
+                               cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--containment", "--config", config,
+                                   "--rules", "3:2", "--seeds", "0-0", "--products", "5", "--out", "bad")]
+                    for result in results:
+                        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr)
+                        self.assertNotIn("Traceback", result.stderr)
+                    self.assertEqual({p.name for p in Path(directory).iterdir()}, {"config.json"})
+        print("P05: +/- 10**400 in all three probability fields; 12 CLI errors exit 2; no output")
+
+    def test_p06_nonfinite_types_and_nearest_valid_boundaries(self):
+        config, _, _, book = load_setup(CONFIG)
+        keys = ("faulty_detection_loss", "defective_lot_probability", "defective_in_lot_probability")
+        invalid = (math.nan, math.inf, -math.inf, True, False, None, "0.5", [], {},
+                   math.nextafter(0.0, -math.inf), math.nextafter(1.0, math.inf))
+        valid = (0, 1, -0.0, math.nextafter(0.0, 1.0), math.nextafter(1.0, 0.0))
+        for key in keys:
+            for value in (*invalid, *valid):
+                with self.subTest(key=key, value=repr(value)):
+                    candidate = {**config, "defective_lot_probability": 0.25, "defective_in_lot_probability": 0.2}
+                    candidate[key] = {"LEAK-2": value} if key == "faulty_detection_loss" else value
+                    if any(value is item for item in invalid):
+                        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+                            check_lot_config(candidate, book)
+                    else:
+                        check_lot_config(candidate, book)
+        print("P06: 33 invalid type/nonfinite/outside-boundary cases rejected; 15 valid boundary cases accepted")
+
+    def test_p07_rejected_configuration_then_valid_run_same_destination(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            config = fixture(directory, faulty_detection_loss={"LEAK-2": -(10 ** 400)})
+            args = ("--config", config, "--seed", "7", "--products", "20", "--out", "gen.csv")
+            bad = cli(directory, "src.generate", *args)
+            self.assertEqual(bad.returncode, 2, bad.stderr)
+            config = fixture(directory, error_rate=0)
+            self.success(cli(directory, "src.generate", *args))
+            self.success(cli(directory, "src.main", Path(directory) / "gen.csv", "--recipe", RECIPE,
+                             "--equipment", EQUIPMENT, "--summary", "--containment"))
+            rows = read_csv(Path(directory) / "reports/gen_rejections.csv")
+            self.assertEqual(len(rows), 8)
+            self.assertTrue(all(row["rejection_count"] == "0" for row in rows), rows)
+            self.assertEqual(json.loads((Path(directory) / "gen.csv.meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["generator_version"], "1.3.0")
+            print("P07: rejected config leaves no partial output; corrected config generates and judges successfully")
+

+class PreservationChecks(unittest.TestCase):
+    def success(self, result):
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+
+    def compare_reports(self, directory, expected):
+        paths = sorted((Path(directory) / "reports").glob("*.csv"))
+        self.assertEqual(len(paths), expected)
+        for path in paths:
+            self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), (ROOT / "reports" / path.name).read_bytes(), path.name)
+        return len(paths)
+
+    def test_p08_protected_tracked_files_unchanged(self):
+        paths = subprocess.check_output(["git", "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", BASE,
+                                         "tests", "src/rules.py", "recipes", "data", "reports"], cwd=ROOT,
+                                        text=True, encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
+        changes = subprocess.check_output(["git", "diff", "--name-only", BASE, "--", *paths], cwd=ROOT,
+                                          text=True, encoding="utf-8")
+        self.assertEqual(changes, "")
+        print("P08: protected pre-stage6 tracked files unchanged:", len(paths))
+

class ScopeTests(Base):
    def t70(self, lots="LLLLLL"):
        names = "abcdef"
        events = prepared(dict(zip(names, lots)))
        for index, (product_id, result) in enumerate(zip(names, ("PASS",) * 4 + ("FAIL",) * 2)):
            events.append(leak(100 + index, product_id, result))
        return events

    def test_t70_methods(self):
        rows = build_containment(self.analysis(self.t70()))
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "window"), ["d"])
        for method in ("last_10", "last_20", "since_restart"):
            self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", method), ["a", "b", "c", "d"])

    def test_t71_restart_splits_the_segment(self):
        events = self.t70() + prepared({"g": "M", "h": "M", "i": "M"})
        events += [restart(200), leak(201, "g", "PASS"), leak(202, "h", "FAIL"), leak(203, "i", "FAIL")]
        rows = build_containment(self.analysis(events))
        for method in ("window", "last_10", "last_20", "since_restart"):
            self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#2", method), ["g"])
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "since_restart"), ["a", "b", "c", "d"])

    def test_t72_lot_scope_uses_fail_lots(self):
        rows = build_containment(self.analysis(self.t70(("L1", "L1", "L2", "L2", "L1", "L2"))))
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "lot"), ["a", "b", "c", "d"])

    def test_t73_lot_scope_can_be_empty(self):
        rows = build_containment(self.analysis(self.t70(("L9",) * 4 + ("L1", "L1"))))
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "lot"), [])
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "since_restart"), ["a", "b", "c", "d"])

    def test_t74_empty_and_mode_cases(self):
        events = prepared({"a": "L", "b": "L"}) + [leak(100, "a", "FAIL"), leak(101, "b", "FAIL")]
"""기존 보고서 묶음과 독립된 전체 입력 분석과 긴 표 summary."""

from collections import Counter, defaultdict
import csv
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP
from pathlib import Path

from .recipe_rules import RESTART_EVENT, RecipeProcess
from .reason_codes import INJECTED_ERROR_CODES, REASON_CODES, rejection_code


ASSUMPTION_NOTICE = "생성 데이터의 확률은 가정이며 실제 설비 진단 근거가 아니다."
SUMMARY_COLUMNS = (
    "section", "key", "value", "numerator", "denominator", "count", "inspection", "equipment", "rule_id",
    "first_count", "first_fail_count", "first_fail_rate", "all_count", "all_fail_count", "all_fail_rate",
    "rejected_count", "unperformed_count", "state", "row_index", "seq", "product_id",
    "cause_row_indices", "cause_seqs", "cause_product_ids", "normal_rows", "injected_count",
    "injection_rate", "actual_rejections", "reason_template",
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InspectionRecord:
    # 1부터 세는 데이터 행 인덱스이며 CSV 헤더는 세지 않는다. seq와 무관하다.
    row_index: int
    seq: str
    product_id: str
    inspection: str
    equipment: str
    result: str
    retest_number: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EquipmentStop:
    equipment: str
    row_index: int
    seq: str
    product_id: str
    fail_causes: tuple
    window: tuple


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EquipmentRestart:
    equipment: str
    row_index: int
    seq: str
    result: str


@dataclass
class Analysis:
    process: RecipeProcess
    events: list
    decisions: list
    first_inspections: list
    all_inspections: list
    stops: dict
    stop_history: list
    restarts: list

    def stop_at(self, equipment, row_index):
        """그 행 시점에 유효했던 정지."""
        return [stop for stop in self.stop_history
                if stop.equipment == equipment and stop.row_index < row_index][-1]


def build_analysis(events, models, equipment_book):
    """입력을 물리 순서로 처리한다. 분석과 trace가 같은 판정·정지 근거를 쓴다."""
    process = RecipeProcess(models, equipment_book)
    events = [dict(event) for event in events]
    decisions, first, all_results, stops = [], [], [], {}
    stop_history, restarts = [], []
    equipment_windows = defaultdict(list)
    for row_index, event in enumerate(events, 1):
        product_before = process.products.get(event.get("product_id"))
        inspection = event.get("inspection")
        is_first = product_before is not None and inspection not in product_before.inspections
        decision = process.apply(event)
        decisions.append(decision)
        if decision.accepted and equipment_book.restart_enabled and event["event"] == RESTART_EVENT:
            equipment_windows[event["equipment"]].clear()
            restarts.append(EquipmentRestart(event["equipment"], row_index, decision.seq, event["result"]))
        if not decision.accepted or event["event"] != "INSPECT":
            continue
        product = process.products[event["product_id"]]
        record = InspectionRecord(
            row_index, decision.seq, decision.product_id, inspection, event["equipment"], event["result"],
            0 if is_first else product.retest_counts[inspection],
        )
        all_results.append(record)
        if not is_first:
            continue
        first.append(record)
        window = equipment_windows[record.equipment]
        window.append(record)
        del window[:-equipment_book.window]
        if process.equipment[record.equipment].stopped_at_seq is not None:
            stop = EquipmentStop(
                record.equipment, row_index, record.seq, record.product_id,
                tuple(item for item in window if item.result == "FAIL"), tuple(window),
            )
            stops.setdefault(record.equipment, stop)
            stop_history.append(stop)
    return Analysis(process, events, decisions, first, all_results, stops, stop_history, restarts)


def _ratio(numerator, denominator):
    if not denominator:
        return ""
    value = Decimal(numerator) / Decimal(denominator)
    return format(value.quantize(Decimal("0.0001"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP), ".4f")


def _row(section, key, **fields):
    return {column: fields.get(column, section if column == "section" else key if column == "key" else "")
            for column in SUMMARY_COLUMNS}


def _metrics(section, key, first, all_results, rejected_count, unperformed_count="", **identity):
    first_fails = sum(item.result == "FAIL" for item in first)
    all_fails = sum(item.result == "FAIL" for item in all_results)
    return _row(
        section, key, first_count=len(first), first_fail_count=first_fails,
        first_fail_rate=_ratio(first_fails, len(first)), all_count=len(all_results),
        all_fail_count=all_fails, all_fail_rate=_ratio(all_fails, len(all_results)),
        rejected_count=rejected_count, unperformed_count=unperformed_count, **identity,
    )

+P07 초안은 `rejections.csv`를 개별 거절 행 목록으로 오해해 빈 목록을 기대했다. 실제 파일은 규칙별 집계 8행이며 모두 0이었다. `src/recipe_reports.py`의 기존 집계 형식을 확인하고 **검수용 반례만** “8행·거절 횟수 전부 0” 단정으로 고쳤다. 제품 코드·기존 테스트·명세는 바꾸지 않았다. 최초 11개 실행 중 다른 10개는 통과했고, 수정한 P07만 별도 재실행해 통과했다. 전체 테스트는 이 반례 수정 후 반복하지 않았다.
+
+## 보존 조건과 Claude 의견 판정
+
+- 기존 8개 테스트 파일과 `src/rules.py`는 5단계 완료 기준과 동일하고, 전체 테스트 실제 실행은 165개 통과했다. `lot_tracking` 없음/false의 보고서 동일성도 기존 T69가 통과했다.
+- 기존 설정·샘플·보고서는 저장된 파일의 무변경만 확인한 것이 아니라, P09·P10에서 임시 폴더에 재생성해 바이트 비교까지 했다. seed42 CSV/meta 동일성으로 기존 생성 난수 흐름의 대표 사례도 확인했다.
+- 반례 후 H1의 빈 집합 초기화 설명에 동의한다. 정지 없는 실행도 실행·유출 분모에서 빠지지 않으며, 비로트의 방식 수와 정지 없는 개별 격리 보고서 출력도 보존된다(P01–P04).
+- “기존 평가에서는 빠진 방식을 이미 빈 집합으로 셌으므로 제출 보고서 값은 바뀌지 않는다”는 Claude의 설명을 받아들인다. 코드 경로뿐 아니라 P11의 제출 보고서 10개 재생성·바이트 일치로 확인했다. 구현자가 재생성하지 못했다는 사실을 미해결 결함으로 세지 않는다.
+- H2에서 범위를 유한성보다 먼저 검사하는 수정에 동의한다. int는 허용 범위 안이면 0·1뿐이므로 OverflowError 경로가 없고, NaN·inf·bool·경계 검사도 유지된다(P05–P07).
+- M1의 meta·보고서 묶음 비교 강화에 동의한다. 실제 두 번의 판정을 거쳐 파일명과 내용 전체를 비교하므로 이전 검증 누락을 해결한다.
+- 이번 수정 기록에는 기존 지적을 기각해 달라는 별도 반박은 없었다. 세 수정의 근거와 기존 보고서 보존 설명을 검증해 모두 해결로 처리했다.
+
+## 테스트 실행 명령과 결과 원문
+
+### 지정 명령 직접 실행
+
+프로세스 환경에만 아래 두 값을 설정했다. 파일·영구 환경은 바꾸지 않았다.
+
+```powershell
+$env:PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1'
+$env:PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8'
+py -m unittest discover -s tests
+```
+
+```text
+No installed Python found!
+```
+
+종료 코드 1. 테스트는 시작되지 않았다. **지정 py 런처를 통한 전체 테스트 실행은 미검증**이다.
+
+```powershell
+py -m unittest handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_013.py
+```
+
+```text
+No installed Python found!
+```
+
+종료 코드 1. 반례도 이 명령에서는 시작되지 않았다.
+
+### 설치된 Python으로 전체 테스트 실제 실행 1회
+
+기존 설치 Python 3.12.10을 사용했다. 테스트 파일·단정은 수정하지 않았으며, 테스트가 시작하는 자식 프로세스의 첫 인자 `py`만 같은 인터프리터로 바꾸는 메모리 내 어댑터를 썼다. 아래 전체 테스트는 한 번만 실행했다. 지정 명령의 성공이나 Python 3.9 검증으로 보고하지 않는다.
+
+```powershell
+$env:PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1'
+$env:PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8'
+@'
+import runpy, subprocess, sys
+original = subprocess.Popen
+class InstalledPython(original):
+    def __init__(self, args, *a, **kw):
+        if isinstance(args, (list, tuple)) and args and args[0] == 'py':
+            args = [sys.executable, *args[1:]]
+        super().__init__(args, *a, **kw)
+subprocess.Popen = InstalledPython
+sys.argv = ['unittest', 'discover', '-s', 'tests']
+runpy.run_module('unittest', run_name='__main__')
+'@ | & 'C:\Users\PC\AppData\Local\Programs\Python\Python312\python.exe' -
+```
+
+```text
+.....................................................................................................................................................................
+----------------------------------------------------------------------
+Ran 165 tests in 131.898s
+
+OK
+T44 seed=42 products=500 generation_seconds=0.170492 judgment_analysis_seconds=0.122259
+T44 seed=7 products=200 generation_seconds=0.087465 judgment_analysis_seconds=0.033842
+T44 seed=42 all_rows_deepcopy_invariant_seconds=61.582899
+T44 seed=7 all_rows_deepcopy_invariant_seconds=7.895771
+```
+
+종료 코드 0. 기존 138개와 6단계 테스트를 포함한 165개 통과.
+

+"""Review 013: focused adversarial checks and required preservation checks.
+
+Run: py -m unittest handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_013.py
+Generated files are confined to TemporaryDirectory; no prior probe is imported.
+"""
+import copy
+import csv
+from dataclasses import replace
+from fractions import Fraction
+import hashlib
+import json
+import math
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+
+from src.containment import METHODS, build_containment, run_scopes
+from src.equipment import load_equipment
+from src.evaluate import containment_metrics
+from src.generate import check_lot_config, generate, load_setup
+from src.recipe import load_recipe
+from src.recipe_rules import LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS
+from src.summary import build_analysis
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+BASE = "d12e92e48c9cfc0bc716d54a39d169120ca581f8"
+RECIPE = ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json"
+EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes/equipment_lot.json"
+CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes/generator_lot.json"
+
+
+def cli(directory, module, *args):
+    return subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, "-m", module, *map(str, args)], cwd=directory,
+        env={**os.environ, "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1",
+             "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"}, capture_output=True, text=True,
+        encoding="utf-8", timeout=300)
+
+
+def read_csv(path):
+    with Path(path).open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
+        return list(csv.DictReader(source))
+
+
+def fixture(directory, **changes):
+    data = json.loads(CONFIG.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+    data.update(recipe=str(RECIPE), equipment=str(EQUIPMENT))
+    data.update(changes)
+    path = Path(directory) / "config.json"
+    path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
+    return path
+
+
+def event(pid, name, result="", lot="", model="", seq=1):
+    return dict(zip(LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS, (str(seq), pid, name,
+                    "LEAK" if name == "INSPECT" else "", result, model,
+                    "LEAK-1" if name == "INSPECT" else "", lot)))
+
+
+def prepared(*ids):
+    return [item for pid in ids for item in (
+        event(pid, "REGISTER", lot="L1", model="B"), event(pid, "P1"), event(pid, "P2"))]
+
+
+class FocusedProbes(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls):
+        cls.models = load_recipe(RECIPE).models
+        cls.book = load_equipment(EQUIPMENT, cls.models)
+
+    def success(self, result):
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+
+    def test_p01_empty_scopes_mode_combinations(self):
+        for lot in (False, True):
+            for restart in (False, True):
+                with self.subTest(lot=lot, restart=restart):
+                    book = replace(self.book, lot_tracking=lot, restart_enabled=restart)
+                    analysis = build_analysis([], self.models, book)
+                    result = run_scopes(analysis)
+                    expected = [m for m in METHODS if lot or m != "lot"]
+                    self.assertEqual(list(result), expected)
+                    self.assertTrue(all(value == set() for value in result.values()))
+                    self.assertEqual(build_containment(analysis), [])
+                    # The empty sets must not share storage across methods or calls.
+                    result["window"].add("sentinel")
+                    self.assertTrue(all(not result[m] for m in expected if m != "window"))
+                    self.assertTrue(all(not s for s in run_scopes(analysis).values()))
+        print("P01: four lot/restart combinations; independent empty sets; no report rows")
+
+    def test_p02_no_stop_with_actual_leaks_retains_denominator(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            config = fixture(directory, error_rate=0, defective_product_probability=1,
+                             defective_fail_probability=0, normal_fail_probability=0,
+                             faulty_equipment={"LEAK-2": 0})
+            self.success(cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--containment", "--config", config,
+                             "--rules", "3:2", "--seeds", "0-1", "--products", "12", "--out", "dev"))
+            rows = read_csv(Path(directory) / "reports/dev_containment.csv")
+            methods = [r for r in rows if r["section"] == "method"]
+            self.assertEqual([r["method"] for r in methods], list(METHODS))
+            for row in methods:
+                self.assertGreater(int(row["leaks"]), 0)
+                self.assertEqual((row["runs"], row["leak_runs"], row["stops"]), ("2", "2", "0"))
+                self.assertEqual((row["capture_rate"], row["precision"], row["mean_quarantine"]),
+                                 ("0.0000", "", "0.00"))
+                self.assertEqual(Fraction(row["mean_residual"]), Fraction(int(row["leaks"]), 2))
+            print("P02: no stops, leaks=" + methods[0]["leaks"] + "; capture=0.0000; residual=" + methods[0]["mean_residual"])
+
+    def test_p03_every_selected_method_without_stops(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            config = fixture(directory, error_rate=0, defective_product_probability=0,
+                             normal_fail_probability=0, faulty_equipment={"LEAK-2": 0})
+            chosen = Path(directory) / "selection.csv"
+            sha = hashlib.sha256(config.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+            for method in METHODS:
+                with self.subTest(method=method):
+                    with chosen.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
+                        writer = csv.DictWriter(target, fieldnames=("section", "method", "value"))
+                        writer.writeheader()
+                        writer.writerows([{"section": "provenance", "method": "config_sha256", "value": sha},
+                                          {"section": "selection", "method": method, "value": "선택"}])
+                    self.success(cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--containment", "--config", config,
+                                     "--rules", "3:2", "--seeds", "1000-1000", "--products", "1",
+                                     "--out", method, "--holdout", "--selected-from", chosen))
+                    rows = read_csv(Path(directory) / "reports" / (method + "_containment.csv"))
+                    self.assertEqual([r["method"] for r in rows if r["section"] == "method"], list(METHODS))
+                    self.assertEqual([(r["method"], r["value"]) for r in rows if r["section"] == "holdout_verdict"],
+                                     [(method, "미확인")])
+                    self.assertFalse(any(r["section"] == "selection" for r in rows))
+            print("P03: all five selected methods exit 0 / unconfirmed; no reselection")
+
+    def test_p04_mixed_runs_rejection_and_scope_purity(self):
+        rows = prepared("a", "b", "c") + [event("a", "INSPECT", "PASS"),
+                event("b", "INSPECT", "FAIL"), event("c", "INSPECT", "FAIL")]
+        stopped = build_analysis(rows, self.models, self.book)
+        no_stop_rows = prepared("d") + [event("d", "INSPECT", "FAIL", lot="bad"),
+                                       event("d", "INSPECT", "PASS")]
+        no_stop = build_analysis(no_stop_rows, self.models, self.book)
+        self.assertEqual([d.accepted for d in no_stop.decisions[-2:]], [False, True])
+        before = copy.deepcopy((no_stop.process.products, no_stop.process.equipment))
+        empty = run_scopes(no_stop)
+        self.assertEqual((no_stop.process.products, no_stop.process.equipment), before)
+        result = containment_metrics([({"a"}, run_scopes(stopped)), ({"d"}, empty)])
+        for method in METHODS:
+            self.assertEqual(result[method]["capture_rate"], Fraction(1, 2))
+            self.assertEqual(result[method]["mean_quarantine"], Fraction(1, 2))
+            self.assertEqual(result[method]["mean_residual"], Fraction(1, 2))
+        print("P04: rejected FAIL does not stop; mixed runs capture/quarantine/residual=1/2; state unchanged")
+
+    def test_p05_huge_signed_integers_in_all_three_probabilities(self):
+        for key in ("faulty_detection_loss", "defective_lot_probability", "defective_in_lot_probability"):
+            for value in (10 ** 400, -(10 ** 400)):
+                with self.subTest(key=key, positive=value > 0), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+                    changes = {"defective_lot_probability": 0.25, "defective_in_lot_probability": 0.2}
+                    changes[key] = {"LEAK-2": value} if key == "faulty_detection_loss" else value
+                    config = fixture(directory, **changes)
+                    results = [cli(directory, "src.generate", "--config", config, "--seed", "0",
+                                   "--products", "5", "--out", "gen.csv"),
+                               cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--containment", "--config", config,
+                                   "--rules", "3:2", "--seeds", "0-0", "--products", "5", "--out", "bad")]
+                    for result in results:
+                        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr)
+                        self.assertNotIn("Traceback", result.stderr)
+                    self.assertEqual({p.name for p in Path(directory).iterdir()}, {"config.json"})
+        print("P05: +/- 10**400 in all three probability fields; 12 CLI errors exit 2; no output")
+
+    def test_p06_nonfinite_types_and_nearest_valid_boundaries(self):
+        config, _, _, book = load_setup(CONFIG)
+        keys = ("faulty_detection_loss", "defective_lot_probability", "defective_in_lot_probability")
+        invalid = (math.nan, math.inf, -math.inf, True, False, None, "0.5", [], {},
+                   math.nextafter(0.0, -math.inf), math.nextafter(1.0, math.inf))
+        valid = (0, 1, -0.0, math.nextafter(0.0, 1.0), math.nextafter(1.0, 0.0))
+        for key in keys:
+            for value in (*invalid, *valid):
+                with self.subTest(key=key, value=repr(value)):
+                    candidate = {**config, "defective_lot_probability": 0.25, "defective_in_lot_probability": 0.2}
+                    candidate[key] = {"LEAK-2": value} if key == "faulty_detection_loss" else value
+                    if any(value is item for item in invalid):
+                        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+                            check_lot_config(candidate, book)
+                    else:
+                        check_lot_config(candidate, book)
+        print("P06: 33 invalid type/nonfinite/outside-boundary cases rejected; 15 valid boundary cases accepted")
+
+    def test_p07_rejected_configuration_then_valid_run_same_destination(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            config = fixture(directory, faulty_detection_loss={"LEAK-2": -(10 ** 400)})
+            args = ("--config", config, "--seed", "7", "--products", "20", "--out", "gen.csv")
+            bad = cli(directory, "src.generate", *args)
+            self.assertEqual(bad.returncode, 2, bad.stderr)
+            config = fixture(directory, error_rate=0)
+            self.success(cli(directory, "src.generate", *args))
+            self.success(cli(directory, "src.main", Path(directory) / "gen.csv", "--recipe", RECIPE,
+                             "--equipment", EQUIPMENT, "--summary", "--containment"))
+            rows = read_csv(Path(directory) / "reports/gen_rejections.csv")
+            self.assertEqual(len(rows), 8)
+            self.assertTrue(all(row["rejection_count"] == "0" for row in rows), rows)
+            self.assertEqual(json.loads((Path(directory) / "gen.csv.meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["generator_version"], "1.3.0")
+            print("P07: rejected config leaves no partial output; corrected config generates and judges successfully")
+
+
+class PreservationChecks(unittest.TestCase):
+    def success(self, result):
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+
+    def compare_reports(self, directory, expected):
+        paths = sorted((Path(directory) / "reports").glob("*.csv"))
+        self.assertEqual(len(paths), expected)
+        for path in paths:
+            self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), (ROOT / "reports" / path.name).read_bytes(), path.name)
+        return len(paths)
+
+    def test_p08_protected_tracked_files_unchanged(self):
+        paths = subprocess.check_output(["git", "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", BASE,
+                                         "tests", "src/rules.py", "recipes", "data", "reports"], cwd=ROOT,
+                                        text=True, encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
+        changes = subprocess.check_output(["git", "diff", "--name-only", BASE, "--", *paths], cwd=ROOT,
+                                          text=True, encoding="utf-8")
+        self.assertEqual(changes, "")
+        print("P08: protected pre-stage6 tracked files unchanged:", len(paths))
+
+    def test_p09_previous_modes_samples_and_seed42_bytes(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            for name, recipe, equipment in (
+                ("sample_events", None, None), ("sample_events_v2", "lamp_models.json", None),
+                ("sample_events_retest", "lamp_models_retest.json", None),
+                ("sample_events_equipment", "lamp_models_retest.json", "equipment.json"),
+                ("sample_events_restart", "lamp_models_retest.json", "equipment_restart.json")):
+                args = [ROOT / "data" / (name + ".csv")]
+                if recipe:
+                    args += ["--recipe", ROOT / "recipes" / recipe]
+                if equipment:
+                    args += ["--equipment", ROOT / "recipes" / equipment]
+                if name == "sample_events_restart":
+                    args += ["--summary", "--trace", "f"]
+                self.success(cli(directory, "src.main", *args))
+            generated = Path(directory) / "gen_seed42.csv"
+            generate(42, 500, generated, ROOT / "recipes/generator.json")
+            for suffix in ("", ".meta.json"):
+                self.assertEqual(Path(str(generated) + suffix).read_bytes(),
+                                 (ROOT / "data/generated" / ("gen_seed42.csv" + suffix)).read_bytes())
+            self.success(cli(directory, "src.main", generated, "--recipe", RECIPE,
+                             "--equipment", ROOT / "recipes/equipment_generated.json", "--summary",
+                             "--trace", "GEN_0005", "--trace", "GEN_0030"))
+            print("P09: existing reports byte-identical:", self.compare_reports(directory, 26), "; CSV/meta: 2")
+
+    def test_p10_previous_evaluation_report_bytes(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            for out, config, seeds, rules, extra in (
+                ("eval_dev", "generator_restart.json", "0-99", "3:2,5:2,5:3,10:3,10:4,20:4", ()),
+                ("eval_holdout", "generator_restart.json", "1000-1099", "3:2", ("--holdout",)),
+                ("eval_baseline", "generator.json", "0-99", "3:2", ("--holdout",))):
+                self.success(cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--config", ROOT / "recipes" / config,
+                                 "--seeds", seeds, "--rules", rules, "--out", out, *extra))
+                print("P10 completed:", out, flush=True)
